Proposed fix: the LT3045 (500 mA) with its negative twin LT3094 (500 mA) for matched soft-start; **pending** their datasheet review (ADI PDFs not retrieved this session) and the maintainers' confirmation, because it touches the audio stage (§7 item 13).
9. **MaskROM gate input (lines 78, 98):** the SN74LVC1G57 low threshold is guaranteed only below 0.35 V at 1.65 V; behind the BAT54 (0.24–0.32 V) the margin is 30–110 mV. The PWRON_N pull-up is set to **20 kΩ** to vcca1v8_pmu (≈ 0.09 mA), which keeps the diode current near 0.1 mA, where the 0.24–0.32 V drop applies.
10. **Debian config re-check:** `GPIO_PCA953X=y` (line 93, was **UNVERIFIED**); `LEDS_LP55XX_COMMON`, `LEDS_LP5521` not set (DKMS).

## Changes in v1.1

Evidence per item in §9.

1. **RK809-5 rail map re-derived (D19)** from the Radxa ROCK 3C v1.4 schematic, whose PMIC is marked RK809-5, and the mainline device trees of three RK3566 + RK809 boards. VCC5–VCC9 (LDOs, BUCK5, both switches) are fed from vcc3v3_sys, which becomes always-on (TLV62569 EN tied to VSYS, EXT_EN unused); VCC1–VCC4 (BUCK1–4) from VSYS. SWOUT1 is 1.5 A (IW612), SWOUT2 3 A (microSD). The RK809-5 OTP defaults remain unpublished.
2. **HAT/USB 5 V boost (D18): TPS61088 → TPS61089RNRR** (line 91). ti.com no longer has a TPS61088 product page or datasheet; the TPS61089 is active.
3. **MCLK supply (D5): TPS7A2033 → TPS7A2031PDBVR (3.1 V)** (line 29), inside the NSC5083D 2.97–3.63 V supply range with 0.4 V headroom at 3.5 V. ADR 0010 amended accordingly. Pull-downs on both ST pins.
4. **RP2354A supplies and clock (D6):** RP_3V3 feeds VREG_VIN, VREG_AVDD, QSPI_IOVDD, USB_OTP_VDD and ADC_AVDD; RP_IOVDD_1V8 feeds IOVDD only. The crystal (line 14) is replaced by a 1.6–3.6 V 12 MHz oscillator on RP_IOVDD_1V8 into XIN.
5. **HAT translator with OE (D8): SN74AXC2T45DCUR → SN74AXC2T245RSWR** (line 95).
6. **MaskROM gate (D10): SN74LVC1G27 → SN74LVC1G57 + SN74LVC1G58** (Schmitt-trigger inputs) (line 98); window specified as 120–350 ms.
7. **HAT 100-pin allocation (D12):** HAT_DET# moves to the 30-pin power connector; 96 of 96 usable contacts on the 100-pin, 25 of 26 on the 30-pin.
8. **Display driver (D14):** mainline has a DSI driver for this exact panel (`panel-ilitek-ili9488`, compatible `focuslcds,e35gh-i-mw800cb`) since v7.3; DKMS on Debian's 7.2. The E35GH stays.
9. **Touch connector (D15):** the Focus drawing gives the CTP tail as 6 contacts, 0.5 mm pitch, 3.50 mm wide, 0.30 mm thick, which is the FH12-6S FPC geometry.
10. **Status LED (D16):** Everlight 19-217/GHC (InGaN green, VF up to 3.7 V) → **19-217/G7C-AN1P2/6T** (AlGaInP yellow-green, VF ≤ 2.4 V) (line 79).
11. **ESD arrays (D17):** TPD6E05U06RVZR (no package drawing in its datasheet) → **TPD4E05U06DQAR** (lines 69, 73, 75); HAT line-level audio on bidirectional TPD1E10B06.
12. **Battery path derated (D24): 2.0 A → 1.6 A continuous** (est.) until JST or Adafruit confirm the PH rating with 26 AWG leads.
13. **Backlight rectifier (D25): PMEG4010CEH → PMEG6010CEH** (60 V, same SOD123F land) (line 53).
14. **HP-amp LDO soft-start (D3)** sized in lines 25–26; **TPS2121 OV1 and thermal (D21)** in line 45.

## 0. Changes from the draft

1. **MCLK oscillators (lines 20, 21): decided in [ADR 0010](../adr/0010-audio-master-clock-source.md)** (3.3 V NDK NSC5083D on a dedicated LDO, passive attenuator into the 1.8 V XTI input). The 1.8 V NSC5083A has no authorized source, and no other available audio-grade 1.8 V oscillator exists at 22.5792 MHz.
2. **Battery (line 46) follows ADR 0007:** Adafruit 328 primary, Adafruit 2011 second source. The made-to-order 105060 pack is removed (T1-038). The 2-pin JST PH connector (line 47) is the single footprint; the board NTC stays.
3. **Battery current budget:** the pack (2C max) and its JST PH lead (2 A per contact) cap the battery path at 2.0 A continuous. VSYS is no longer rated 3.5 A on battery; HAT and USB-C source power on battery are software-budgeted (power tree §4.6).
4. **New HAT/USB 5 V boost (new line 91, TPS61088):** the BQ25895 cannot run OTG while it is charging, so the draft could not power a class A/B HAT while USB-C is plugged in. HAT 5 V and USB-C source power now come from a VSYS boost behind the eFuses.
5. **TPS2121 input isolation (line 45):** both OV inputs are driven during any 5 V sourcing, otherwise the T1's own 5 V re-enters the charger through IN1 or IN2.
6. **vdd_cpu buck (line 3): RK860-2 → RK860-0**, the variant on the RK3566 reference (Radxa ZERO 3W).
7. **IO domains corrected to the pin plan:** PMUIO2 (I2C0/1/2) at 1.8 V, faceplate bank VCCIO5 at 3.3 V. The draft §4.2 had I2C0 at 3.3 V and the faceplate at 1.8 V, which contradicts the pin plan and the DAC's 1.8 V-only I2C on the same PMUIO2 bank.
8. **Faceplate connector: FH35C-45S → FH35C-51S** (44 used + 7 spare = 14 %, ADR 0006).
9. **HAT flat cable (line 66) picked:** Hirose DF40 0.4 mm board-to-FPC pair, 100-pin signal + 30-pin power.
10. **Missing parts added:** GPIO expander TCA6416A, HAT ID translator PCA9306, HAT audio translators (SN74AXC8T245, SN74AXC4T774, SN74AXC2T45), MaskROM combo circuit (TPS3839 + SN74LVC1G27 + BAT54 + DMG1012T), faceplate load switch FP_3V3, panel RST/TE translators on the faceplate, 4.4 mm fallback jack as primary.
11. **ESD counts corrected:** faceplate DSI + low-speed and HAT low-speed lines were under-counted.
12. **4.4 mm jack (line 31):** the G-Switch part becomes primary (T1-029); genuine Pentaconn stays a made-to-order upgrade.
13. **Second sources removed when incompatible:** INA226 (I2C VIH too high for a 1.8 V bus), Alps/Panasonic side switches against a top-actuated primary.
14. **Item tracking (BRD-030) has no hardware line yet:** new *Open* line 103.

## 1. What changed from v0

1. **Master clocks: 45.1584/49.152 MHz → 22.5792/24.576 MHz, 1.8 V.** The CS43198 accepts an external MCLK only at 22.5792 or 24.576 MHz (DS1156F2 Table 4-3), and its XTI/MCLK input is in the 1.8 V VL domain (VL 1.66–1.94 V). BRD-023 is corrected (Confirmed). See power tree §1.
2. **Battery line replaced** by the off-the-shelf Adafruit 328 (ADR 0007). The v0 Jauch LP705176JS fits no envelope.
3. **HAT pogo pins move to the device-side piece** (board ADR 0002, flat cable). The board now carries the flat-cable connector instead; the pogo line is kept for traceability.
4. **eFuses ×2 → ×3:** a third TPS259470 limits and protects the USB-C VBUS source path (OTG host).
5. **New support parts** around every main IC: vdd_cpu buck, LPDDR4X 0.6 V LDO, external 3.3 V buck, audio rails (TPS65131, TPS7A4901, TPS7A3001, TPS62840, TPS7A2018), 5 V boost, load switches, backlight driver, current monitors, ESD/TVS, translators, GPIO expander, crystals, inductors, connectors, buttons and test access.

## 2. Bill of materials

### 2.1 Core

| # | Subsystem | Function | Part (MPN) | Qty | Status | Driver path | Sourcing | Second source | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Core | SoC | Rockchip RK3566 | 1 | Decided (ADR 0003) | mainline + Debian | No authorized channel | Upgrades: RK3576, i.MX 8M Plus | v0 #1 |
| 2 | Core | PMIC | Rockchip RK809-5 | 1 | Proposed | mainline + Debian (`rk8xx`, `rk808-regulator`, `rtc-rk808`, `clk-rk808`) | No authorized channel | RK817-5 | v0 #2. v1.1 rail map from the Radxa ROCK 3C v1.4 schematic (RK809-5) and the mainline Orange Pi 3B, ROCK 3C and ODROID-M1S device trees (power tree §3): VCC1–VCC4 (BUCK1–4) from VSYS (v1.5: VSYS_LV 3.8 V, line 113; the 2.7–5.5 V input cannot take a 2S VSYS), VCC5–VCC9 (LDO1–9, BUCK5, SWOUT1, SWOUT2) from vcc3v3_sys. RK809-5 OTP defaults **UNVERIFIED** (not public). Codec and class-D unused (RK817 codec driver is Debian `=m`, an option for the speaker) v1.9 (pinout v1 B12; HDG p. 73): codec unused: MCLK, BCLK, LRCLK, SDI, PDMCLK and HP_SNS tied to ground; SDO/PDMDATA, CPn/CPp, VCC_CPVDD/CPVSS, HPL/HPR_OUT, SPK_OUTn/p and MIC1p/n may float. SWOUT1 is unused from v1.9 (radio AVDD33 moves to a TPS22919, line 50). |
| 3 | Core | vdd_cpu buck (I2C DVS) | TI TPS6286910CRQYR (TPS62869, 6 A, VQFN-HR-9 1.5 × 2.5 × 1.0 mm) | 1 | Decided (ADR 0021; v1.12, was Rockchip RK860-0, Proposed) | mainline + Debian (`tps6286x`, `ti,tps62869`, `REGULATOR_TPS6286X=m`) | Authorized, single | TPS628691ARQYR (same land; 86.6 kΩ 1 % from pin 9 to AGND: 0.90 V boot, address 0x41); onsemi FAN53555BUC18X (another land, `fan53555`) | v1.12 (ADR 0021): on I2C0 (PMUIO2, 1.8 V; VIH 0.84 V, SLVSFS3B p. 7) at **0x42**; factory boot voltage 0.9 V (p. 15, Table 8-3); VIN from VSYS_LV 3.8 V (line 113) inside 2.4–5.5 V (p. 5); output 0.85–1.15 V in 5 mV steps (mainline `rk3566.dtsi` operating points). EN from vcc3v3_pmu through 51 kΩ + 100 nF (Radxa ZERO 3W); EN low resets every register (p. 14), so EN stays high and vdd_cpu stays on in suspend. PG (pin 9, C option) to a test pad only, never to a reset: it falls during a DVS step down of more than 9 %. VOS senses at the 22 µF under the SoC (HDG 1.1 p. 38). Passives: line 147. BRD-049: 3 A (est.) = 50 % of 6 A; inductor peak 3.76 A = 54 % of the 7 A minimum high-side limit ([Changes in v1.12](#changes-in-v112) item 5). Open on the first boards: the C option regulates from VOUT Register 1; the load step at the SoC; vdd_cpu ready before RESETn (ADR 0021). Was (v1.0–v1.11): Rockchip RK860-0 (no public datasheet) |

IOVDD at 1.8 V (RP_IOVDD_1V8) so it can tap the 1.8 V I2S lines without translators. v1.1: QSPI_IOVDD (internal flash), USB_OTP_VDD, VREG_VIN with VREG_AVDD (ramp together), and ADC_AVDD on RP_3V3 (DS Table 1688, §6.1.8). Firmware sets bank-0 VOLTAGE_SELECT = 1 at boot (DS §6.1.1) v1.9 (pinout v1 C6, C20, B15): BOOTSEL is the QSPI chip select, powered from QSPI_IOVDD = RP_3V3; the SoC pulls it low through an N-FET (DMG1012T-7, line 35) whose gate is `RP_BOOTSEL_DRV` (GPIO3_C6, 1.8 V), so no 3.3 V reaches a 1.8 V ball. RUN has a 4.7 kΩ pull-up to RP_IOVDD_1V8 and the SoC pulls it low (GPIO4_C1, open drain). UART0 and `RP_IRQ` use FT GPIO0–25. |

SLUSDV2C §8.2.2.2: 1.5 MHz works only with 1 µH (750 kHz only with 2.2 µH); Isat must exceed the maximum converter current plus half the ripple, and be at least 2 A for PFM pulses. TI's EVM uses a TDK SPM6530T-1R0M120. Worst case at the 5 A ISW rating: ripple at D = 0.5 (VBUS 12.6 V, the TPS2121 OV1 maximum; VSYS 6.3 V; L −20 %) = 6.3 V × 0.5 / (0.8 µH × 1.5 MHz) = 2.6 A, so the peak is 5 + 1.3 = 6.3 A = 72 % of the 8.7 A Isat (30 % drop, Coilcraft Doc 806). At the T1 design point (IINLIM 2.0 A, ≤ 15 W) the peak is ≈ 3.5 A (40 %, est.). Irms 6.7 A (20 °C rise) against ≤ 5 A |

VC2 to BAT+ and VC1 to the mid-cell tap through the battery contacts (line 47; v1.10, ADR 0020) for per-cell voltage; SRP/SRN across the sense resistor (line 43; coulomb counter ±100 mV, §7.11); TS1 to the board NTC (line 48). I2C1 (1.8 V): VIH = 0.7 × VREG, with the internal LDO at 1.6–2.0 V: 1.12–1.40 V (§7.9, §7.26), met by a 1.8 V pull-up with ≥ 0.4 V margin; 7-bit address 0x55 (TRM SLUUC81 §12.1, p. 57). 250 µA normal, 100 µA sleep, 0.5–2 µA shutdown (§7.5). Gauge only (updated 2026-10-24, SLUUC81 January 2020; §7 item 22): Mfg Status init 0x0008 (GAUGE_EN = 1, FET_EN = 0, PF_EN = 0; gauging is off by default), I2C Configuration 0x00 (the default BCAST = 1 would broadcast as a bus master), Balancing Configuration 0x00, DA Configuration 0x11 (2S, sleep on), firmware protections and PF disabled, CC Gain calibrated per board (SLUUC81). CHG and DSG open, PBI 2.2 µF to VSS. **v1.8, [ADR 0017](../adr/0017-fuel-gauge-wiring-sense-resistor-and-configuration.md) (Accepted):** VC2 and PBI on the pack side of the ship FET (line 120), after the 5 A fuse; VC1 to the mid-cell tap, both through the SLUSE07B Figure 9-1 RC filters; **PACK on the BQ25798 BAT node** (the switched side of the ship FET), through the Figure 9-1 PACK network, not on BAT+: when the ship FET opens, PACK falls below the 3.0 V Charger Present Threshold and the gauge can shut down. Storage and critical-battery power-off (Blackbird OS, no adapter present): gauge MAC 0x0010 twice, then BQ25798 SDRV_CTRL = 01 (shutdown mode, wake on adapter): board drain ≈ 1 µA typical, 4.7 µA maximum (ADR 0017 item 3). Golden image adds (ADR 0017 item 5): the three conflicting TRM fields set explicitly after a read-back check (Enabled Protections C with PTO and CTO cleared, AFE Protection Control RSNS = 0 with RSTRIM as read, IT Gauging Configuration functional bits 0x14CE), DA Configuration 0x19, Power Configuration 0x00, Charger Present Threshold 3000 mV, Shutdown Voltage 2300 mV / 10 s, Ship FET Off Time 10 s and Delay 20 s, Sleep Current 10 mA, Bus Timeout 5 s, AOLD Threshold 0xF4 (6.5 A at 3 mΩ). Chemistry ID and design capacity per pack (golden image, production step) v1.9 (pinout v1 C25, B7): no alert line to the expander: the BQ28Z610-R1 has no alert pin (SLUSE07B p. 3, Figure 6-1); the gauge is read over I2C1 and charger events arrive on `CHG_INT_N`. |

With both ports at their maximum trip the buck carries at most 2.52 A, 84 % of its 3 A rating (was 3.96 A, 132 %); #D's minimum trip is above #A's and #C's maximum (1.981 A, 98 %), so an overload on one port while the other is idle is cut by that port's own eFuse; #D acts on the sum. 1.6 A design = 79 % of #D's minimum trip; switch 46 % of 5.5 A; 72 mW at 1.6 A (RON 28.3 mΩ). EN with the buck's output (UVLO divider from its input); fault to expander P16 (`EFUSE_5V_FLT_N`); IMON to a test pad |

Switch limit 0.56–0.84 A at Dmax; worst-case maximum output with 22 µH (line 52) 82 mA at a 16.5 V string (73 % at 60 mA), 75 mA at 18 V (80 %), 61 mA at 22 V (99 %): a faceplate with a string above 18 V declares a lower full-scale current (Changes in v1.11, faceplate item 4). Was (v1.1–v1.10): TPS61169DCKR, 37.5 V OVP, RSET 3.40 Ω |

Peak 0.44 A at a 16.5 V string and 60 mA = 82 % of the 0.54 A rating (Murata sheet not retrieved, **UNVERIFIED**). The switch limit (0.84 A max) is above the rating, as in TI's own recommended inductors (510–750 mA, §8.2.1.2.2 Table 4): saturation only at start-up into a fault. Was: LQH3NPN100MJRL 10 µH |

Shunts 10 / 100 / 100 / 20 mΩ (§5). v1.2: VSYS shunt 10 mΩ ≥ 1 W (2512): 0.36 W at the 6 A BATFET rating (36 %), 0.23 W at 4.8 A; HAT shunt 20 mΩ ≥ 0.5 W (0.19 W at the 3.1 A boost input, 0.06 W at the 1.66 A eFuse ceiling). v1.5 (2S): the VSYS shunt carries 2.4 A at the full budget (58 mW, 6 %), still 0.36 W at the BQ25798's 6 A continuous rating; the HAT shunt sits at the 5.1 V buck input, ≈ 1.3 A at 6.6 V (est.), 35 mW. Bus voltage up to 8.8 V is inside the INA236 range. For T1-026 instrumentation. v1.8 (G11): positions fixed (power tree §4.12): VSYS 10 mΩ (unchanged); **audio** on a dedicated VSYS_LV branch feeding the TPS65131, TPS62840, TPS7A2031 and the DAC_VP switch, shunt **100 → 20 mΩ** WSL1206R0200FEA (the boost draws ≈ 1.5 A at the positive LDO pair's maximum trip, beyond the 0.82 A full scale of 100 mΩ; 20 mΩ gives 4.1 A); **display** on the TPS61169 VIN, 100 mΩ (0.33 A max = 40 % of full scale); HAT 20 mΩ at the 5.1 V buck input. The LMR33630C input (VSYS) gets a fifth monitor (line 136). Fitted in every variant (ADR 0013) v1.11: the display shunt sits on the TPS61160 VIN (line 51) |

Module supply ranges and sequencing between the rails **UNVERIFIED** (datasheet not obtained). One measurement link per rail (line 137). Suspend draw **UNVERIFIED** until measured on M-RF; `WL_PDN` hold through suspend is §7 item 40. The 802.15.4 SPI (pins 4–8) stays unconnected: the SPI2 M0 balls are unassigned spares reserved for a variant (T1-077 keeps 802.15.4 disabled while not wired). |

| 91 | Power | HAT_5V / USB-C source 5.1 V buck from VSYS | TI TPS62903RPJR (3 A) | 1 | Decided (ADR 0014; v1.5: TPS61089RNRR → TPS62903RPJR) | none | As line 38 | None checked: TPS62902 (2 A) would run at 80 % | v1.5, SLVSES3A. A boost cannot work from a 2S VSYS (VIN above VOUT), so the 5.1 V source is a buck. FB/VSET 95.3 kΩ → 5.1 V (Table 7-2); MODE/S-CONF 26.1 kΩ (VSET, 2.5 MHz, auto PFM/PWM with AEE, output discharge on, Table 7-1). Design output 1.6 A: the full output budget, 1.5 A shared by HAT_5V and the USB-C source, on adapter and on battery (ADR 0014, power tree §4.6). BRD-049: 1.6 A = 53 % of 3 A. With both eFuses at their 1.66 A ceilings (a software fault, 3.3 A) the high-side limit (4.0 / 4.6 / 5.5 A) bounds the fault. 100 % mode: holds 5.1 V at 1.6 A down to VIN ≈ 5.1 + 1.6 × (111 mΩ maximum RDS(on) + 9 mΩ DCR) = 5.29 V, below the 6.0 V VSYS minimum. Unlike the TPS61089 boost, the output does not follow VIN when disabled. Thermal: ≈ 0.6 W loss at 8.16 W out (η 0.93, est.) → ≈ +44 °C at the EVM's 73.5 °C/W (97.2 °C/W JEDEC, §6.4): copper and placement to be checked in the board thermal model. CIN 10 µF 25 V; COUT one 22 µF with ≥ 12 µF effective at 5.1 V (line 121, v1.6; SLVSES3A §6.3 minimum 10 µF). Phase margin at VSYS 6.0 V ≈ 38–40° (generic model corrected, est.): bench item (§7 item 27). EN from `HAT5V_EN` OR `USB_SRC_ON`. Inductor on line 92 **v1.13 (RFC 0014):** eFuse #A and #C at RILM 1.87 kΩ: both at their 1.981 A maximum trip draw 3.96 A, 132 % of the 3 A rating and 99 % of the 4.0 A minimum high-side limit, a software-fault case (§7 item 46). `HAT5V_EN` is now the HAT port's TCPM `vbus-supply` enable **v1.14 (RFC 0015):** eFuse #D (line 44) limits both ports together to 2.520 A at most: **84 %** of the 3 A rating and 63 % of the 4.0 A minimum high-side limit in the double-fault case (§7 item 46 closed); inductor peak 2.52 + 0.54 = 3.06 A, 35 % of the 8.8 A Isat |

TPS62903 nominal 1 µH, listed in SLVSES3A Table 8-4. The ≈ 5.7 A maximum dynamic limit stays below 8.8 A. Irms 8.8 A (20 °C rise) against 1.6 A; 9.0 mΩ maximum DCR → 23 mW |

VCCI 1.8 V (I2C2), VCCP 3.3 V; 0x21. Power-up defaults keep every enable off (BRD-019). v1.2: `CAM_PWDN` dropped from the HAT interface, one port line freed. v1.4: interface v1 drops `GPIO5`–`GPIO7` and `CAM_RST#`, so no HAT line is left on the expander (five lines spare). Debian `GPIO_PCA953X=y` v1.8: **VCCI on vcca1v8_pmu** (G15: vcc_1v8 is off in suspend while the expander holds enables); two of the five spare lines become `HAPTIC_EN` (G20, line 123) and `NFC_PWR_EN` (G7, line 50), each with a board pull-down (P04 and P05 in pinout v1). The PN7160 VEN and DWL_REQ are not on the expander: its ports are 3.3 V and those pins are 1.8 V v1.9 (pinout v1 §3, B11), port map: P00 `FP_IO_EN`, P01 `HAT3V3_EN` (moved from a SoC GPIO), P02 `HAT5V_EN` (= `HAT_SRC_ON`), P03 `USB_SRC_ON`, P04 `HAPTIC_EN`, P05 `NFC_PWR_EN`, **P06 `WL_PWR_EN`** (radio power, decision 2026-10-24; a spare in pinout v1, for the pinout owner to record), P07 spare; P10 `HP_DET_SE`, P11 `HP_DET_BAL`, P12 `FP_DET_N`, P13 `FP_GPIO0`, P14 `FP_GPIO1`, P15–P17 eFuse #A, #B, #C faults. Outputs P00–P06 carry board pull-downs to their off state; `EXP_INT_N` on GPIO0_D6 (PMUIO0). Every output holds through suspend. **v1.13 (RFC 0014):** P01 → `HAT_VBUS_DISCH` (HAT `VBUS` discharge gate, pull-down), P16 → spare; P02 `HAT5V_EN` is the HAT port's TCPM `vbus-supply` enable |

VDD and SENSE on **vcca1v8_pmu** (always on, also in suspend), SENSE divider **280 kΩ / 100 kΩ** (1 %) → trip at 0.405 V × 3.8 = 1.54 V; **CT 390 nF** X7R ±10 % → td = CT / 175 nF + 0.5 ms = 2.23 s typ, **1.21–3.43 s** (spread scaled from the 180 nF row: **UNVERIFIED** that it scales); RESET open-drain with a **100 kΩ** pull-up to vcca1v8_pmu; MR open (internal 90 kΩ pull-up). VDD minimum 1.7 V (−40 to 125 °C): vcca1v8_pmu at −5 % is 1.71 V. Simulated: D0 held 1.20–3.42 s after RESETB, never at resume, normal boot untouched. Replaces the v1.1 window (TPS3839 on vcc_1v8, 120–350 ms), which was too short to be guaranteed and reopened at every resume (§9 D10, simulation §1 items 12–13). **Consequence:** MaskROM through the buttons needs a full power-off (a warm restart keeps vcca1v8_pmu up and does not reopen the window) and an empty or unbootable SD slot (a bootable SD precedes USB download; BootROM order **UNVERIFIED**); software can still reboot into the loader |

Doc 1529-1: DCR 13.0 / 14.3 mΩ, Isat 3.2 / 5.3 / 7.5 A at 10 / 20 / 30 % drop, Irms 8.0 A (20 °C rise). v1.6 correction ([VSYS buck and MaskROM simulations](../../sim/README.md)): SLVSDV2 Eq. 10 (100 ns) understates the ripple; the on-time of Eq. 1 (250 ns × VIN / (VIN − VOUT)) gives ΔIL = 250 ns × VIN / L, confirmed by TI's TPS62136 model. Peak at 2.0 A, VSYS 9.9 V (OVP), L −20 %: ΔIL = 2.06 A → **3.03 A** = **63 %** of the 4.8 A minimum high-side limit and **40 %** of 7.5 A Isat (30 % drop; 57 % of 5.3 A at 20 %); the 6.5 A maximum high-side limit stays below 7.5 A. Still compliant. |

CSD17575Q3 (SLPS489A, Aug 2014): VDS 30 V (2S pack ≤ 8.8 V: 29 %), VGS ±20 V (≈ 5 V drive: 25 %), ID 60 A package limit, 27 A continuous at RθJA 45 °C/W; RDS(on) 3.2 mΩ max at 4.5 V. Current margin: 27 A continuous is **6.8×** the 4 A of the brief and 8.4× the 3.2 A worst case (≥ 2× required); 3.2 A → 33 mW at 3.2 mΩ. IGSS ≤ 100 nA, IDSS ≤ 1 µA at 24 V. SDRV no longer gets the 1 nF-to-GND of the unused case (line 115). v1.8 ([ADR 0017](../adr/0017-fuel-gauge-wiring-sense-resistor-and-configuration.md)): the ship FET also switches the gauge's PACK sense (line 42); only the gauge (VC1, VC2, PBI, SRP/SRN), the BQ25798 BATP sense and this FET sit on its pack side. Storage and critical-battery power-off use the charger's **shutdown mode** (SDRV_CTRL = 01, ISD_BAT 0.5 / 0.7 µA), not ship mode (SDRV_CTRL = 10, 11 / 16 µA); IDSS ≤ 1 µA counts in the ≈ 1 / 4.7 µA storage drain |

VIN = VSYS_LV 3.8 V (line 113). R1 200 kΩ / R2 100 kΩ 1 % → 0.6 V × 3 = 1.80 V (Eq. 4; VFB 594–606 mV → 1.78–1.82 V before resistor tolerance), C3 120 pF (12 µ / R2, Eq. 5). Load (E2B-09-1413 Tables 18–21): 390 mA typical (802.15.4 TX 13 dBm), 323–345 mA (Bluetooth TX), 255 mA (Wi-Fi 5 GHz TX); start-up calibration peak above 1 A (EA M.2 PA4 pp. 8, 22, carrier): **1.05 A design peak**. BRD-049: 390 mA = 13 % of 3 A; 1.05 A peak = 35 %. Ripple at 1.8 V / 3.8 V, 0.47 µH, 2.2 MHz: ΔIL = 1.8 × (1 − 1.8 / 3.8) / (0.47 µH × 2.2 MHz) = 0.92 A → peak 1.51 A = **41 %** of the 3.7 A minimum high-side limit. **EN tied to vcc3v3_wl** (SWOUT1; EN VIH 1.0 V, rated to 6 V), so AVDD33 and AVDD18 switch together on one RK809 control; a DNP 0 Ω from a GPIO allows separate control if the module datasheet asks for an order (AVDD33/AVDD18 sequencing **UNVERIFIED**). Variant: fit in `full` and `production` v1.9: EN still follows vcc3v3_wl, now the output of the line 50 switch on expander P06 (`WL_PWR_EN`), so AVDD33 and AVDD18 stay on together in suspend when tracking is enabled. |

| 133 | Audio | DAC control-bus isolation (I2C2 SDA/SCL to both CS43198; G2) | TI SN74LVC2G66DCUR (dual SPST bilateral analog switch, VSSOP-8) | 1 | Proposed (new, v1.8) | none | Authorized, multiple | Nexperia 74LVC2G66DC,125 (pin-compatibility **UNVERIFIED** until its data sheet is read); SN74LVC2G66DCTR | SCES325N (Aug 2018): VCC 1.65–5.5 V; ron at VCC 1.65 V, 4 mA: 12.5 typ / 30 Ω max, peak 85 typ / 120 Ω max; control VIH 0.65 × VCC. **VCC on vcca1v8_pmu, both control inputs on DAC_DIG_1V8**: the switch closes only while the DACs are powered, and opens with no software step. 4.7 kΩ pull-ups on the DAC side (DAC_DIG_1V8). A DAC pulling the bus low through the switch carries the bus pull-up current: with I2C2's 2.2 kΩ (§5) that is 0.82 mA × 120 Ω = 98 mV on top of the DAC's VOL, a thin margin to VIL = 0.3 × 1.8 V = 0.54 V (CS43198 VOL at that current not checked, **UNVERIFIED**); with 4.7 kΩ bus pull-ups it is 46 mV. The I2C2 pull-up value is set at schematic capture against the bus rise time (BOM §7 item 34). TCA9406 (the gap's first proposal) needs VCCB ≥ 2.3 V (its data sheet, pin table) and cannot isolate 1.8 V to 1.8 V. **0 Ω bypass footprints** (DNP) across both switches: fit them, and leave the switch off, only if the CS43198 control pins prove fail-safe unpowered (**UNVERIFIED**). DAC_INT pull-up on DAC_DIG_1V8, never on an always-on rail. Block: host side of `blk.audio` (absent-state parts live outside the block). Variant: fit in every variant |

With 0 Ω fitted the hardware is unchanged; a series value bounds injection into an unpowered DAC if bring-up shows it (driver rule stays: park I2S low before DAC_DIG_1V8 drops, power tree §5). RP2350 FT GPIO absolute maximum is 3.63 V with IOVDD = 0 V (RP2350 datasheet), so 1.8 V taps into an unpowered RP2354A stay inside it if the taps use GPIO0–GPIO25 (FT), never GPIO26–29 (pinout v1 G4 confirms it); injection current into an unpowered FT pad is not specified (**UNVERIFIED**). ## 4. Protection, level shifting and clocks

### 4.1 ESD capacitance budget for high-speed lines

A shunt capacitance C on a line of characteristic impedance Z0 sees the two line halves in parallel, so its time constant is τ = (Z0/2)·C, and it adds a 10–90 % edge of about 2.2·τ.

| Interface | Z0 single-ended | Unit interval | C per line | 2.2·τ | Share of UI | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| USB 3 Gen 1 (5 Gb/s; PCIe Gen 2 until v1.12) | 45 Ω (90 Ω diff) | 200 ps | 0.5 pF (TPD4E05U06 typ) | 25 ps | 12 % | OK |
| same | 45 Ω | 200 ps | 1.0 pF | 50 ps | 25 % | Marginal; avoid |
| CSI-2 D-PHY (2.5 Gb/s per lane max) | 50 Ω | 400 ps | 0.8 pF (TPD4E1U06) | 44 ps | 11 % | OK; TPD4E05U06 used anyway for one part number. v1.13: no HAT CSI-2 (RFC 0014) |
| USB 2.0 HS (480 Mb/s) | 45 Ω | 2.08 ns | 0.5 pF | 25 ps | 1 % | OK |
| MIPI DSI to the faceplate (1 lane, ≤ 500 Mb/s) | 50 Ω | ≥ 2 ns | 0.5 pF | 28 ps | 1.4 % | OK |
| MIPI DSI, other faceplates (4 lanes, ≤ 1 Gb/s per lane, **UNVERIFIED** RK3566 maximum) | 50 Ω | 1 ns | 0.5 pF | 28 ps | 2.8 % | OK |
| Headphone outputs (audio) | — | — | 12 pF (TPD1E10B06) | — | — | 12 pF against the ≈ 0.5 Ω output impedance of a paralleled pair (1.0 Ω ballast per half, v1.3) is a 27 GHz pole: irrelevant |

The add-on connectors (flat cable, pogo contact) and the passive 2:1 muxes of the topology report add their own loss and capacitance; the ESD part is the smallest term at 0.5 pF. v1.13 (RFC 0014): the HAT extension and its switch are removed. v1.11: the HAT extension passes the TMUXHS4412 (line 144), 0.45 pF on-capacitance and −0.7 dB at 2.5 GHz typical (SLASEW5A §§5.5, 5.6).

### 4.2 IO domains and level shifting

Corrected to the [pinout v1](pinout-v1.md). The draft table had I2C0 at 3.3 V and the faceplate at 1.8 V; both contradict the pin plan, and I2C0, I2C1 and I2C2 M0 sit in the same PMUIO2 bank as the CS43198 control port, whose VL-domain pins tolerate only VL + 0.3 V (≈ 2.1–2.2 V, DS1156F2 absolute maxima).

v1.9 (pinout v1 B13): the table follows the IO-domain map of [pinout v1](pinout-v1.md) §1, which re-read every domain on the RK3566 datasheet 1.1 and its hardware design guide; the v1–v1.8 table from pin multiplexing v0 is superseded. A domain's supply and its `pmu_io_domains` setting must match (HDG pp. 30–31); the rail per domain is in power tree §3.1.

| Domain | Voltage | Rail | Devices and lines | Level handling |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PMUIO0 | 1.8 V (fixed) | vcca1v8_pmu | `TPM_RST_N`, `NFC_FD_N`, `NFC_IRQ`, `EXP_INT_N` (always on, wake-capable) | none |
| PMUIO1 | 3.3 V (fixed) | vcc3v3_pmu | `PMIC_INT`, `PMIC_SLEEP`, `TSADC_SHUT`, nPOR, `FLASH_VOL_SEL`, SD detect, `HAT_PD_INT_N` (v1.13; `HAT_DET#`, `HAT_INT#` until v1.12), `FP_HID_INT_N` | none |
| PMUIO2: I2C0 | 1.8 V | vcca1v8_pmu | RK809 0x20, TPS62869 0x42 (v1.12, ADR 0021; RK860-0 0x40 before) | RK809 at 1.8 V as on Radxa ZERO 3 and Quartz64-B; TPS62869 VIH 0.84 V, SDA/SCL rated to 6 V (SLVSFS3B pp. 5, 7) |
| PMUIO2: I2C1 | 1.8 V | vcca1v8_pmu | FUSB302B 0x22, FUSB302B01MPX 0x23 (HAT port, v1.13), INA236 ×4 0x40–0x43 and INA236B 0x48, BQ28Z610-R1 0x55, BQ25798 0x6B | FUSB302B VIH 1.32 V (FUSB302B/D Rev. 5 p. 16), BQ25798 VIH_SDA 1.3 V, BQ28Z610-R1 VIH ≤ 1.40 V, INA236 1.2 V-compliant |
| PMUIO2: I2C2 M0 | 1.8 V | vcca1v8_pmu | TCA6416A 0x21, PN7160 0x28, TPM 0x2E, CS43198 0x30 and 0x31 (behind line 133), LP5521 0x32, AT24C02D 0x50, NTAG I2C plus 0x55, DRV2605L 0x5A, LSM6DSV16X 0x6A | Pull-ups on vcca1v8_pmu; every device always on or isolated (G16); CS43198 VIH = 0.7 × VL; LP5521 VIH = 0.8 × V(EN), EN on vcca1v8_pmu |
| PMUIO2: UART0, UART2, wake inputs | 1.8 V | vcca1v8_pmu | RP2354A UART0 and `RP_IRQ` (FT pins), debug UART, `ACTION_BTN_N`, `CHG_INT_N`, `USBC_INT_N`, `IMU_INT1`, `WL_WAKE_OUT`, `BT_WAKE_OUT`, `CLK32K_PMIC` | 1.8 V debug adapter |
| VCCIO1 | 3.3 V | vcc3v3_sys | `RP_PWR_EN`, spares (v1.13: the HAT lines are freed, RFC 0014) | none |
| VCCIO2 | 1.8 V (`FLASH_VOL_SEL` high) | vcc_1v8 | eMMC HS200, `TPM_PIRQ_N` (v1.13: `HAT_AUD_OE_N` removed) | none |
| VCCIO3 | 1.8/3.3 V | vccio_sd (RK809 LDO5) | microSD UHS-I | in-bank switch |
| VCCIO4 | 1.8 V | vcc_1v8 | Type 2EL SDIO 3.0 and Bluetooth UART, `WL_PDN`, `BT_WAKE_IN`, PN7160 `NFC_VEN` and `NFC_DWL_REQ`; four SPI2 M0 balls unassigned (reserved for a variant, v1.9) | none (v1.13: the PCA9306 is removed) |
| VCCIO5 | 3.3 V | vcc3v3_sys | Faceplate I2C3 (GT911 0x5D/0x14, VEML6030 0x10, HID controller), SPI1/DBI, touch and panel lines, `FP_ALS_INT_N`, speaker I2S3 and `SPK_SD_MODE`, `BL_PWM` | I2C3 and interrupt pull-ups on FP_3V3 (G8); faceplates with 1.8 V IOVCC translate on their side (line 96) |
| VCCIO6 | 1.8 V | vccio6_1v8 (vcc_1v8 through the line 141 link) | I2S1 (DACs, DSD; the HAT audio port is removed in v1.13), PDM, audio and amplifier enables, `DAC_RST_N`, `DAC_INT_N`, RP2354A SWD, RUN and BOOTSEL drive | none (v1.13: the AXC translators are removed); DAC nets untranslated |
| VCCIO7 | 3.3 V | vcc3v3_sys | `MCLK441_EN` and `MCLK48_EN` (ST, VIH ≥ 2.17 V), `HPA_RAIL_EN`, `HPA_LDO_EN`, spares | none: native 3.3 V balls, no translator or expander line |

### 4.3 Clocks

| Clock | Part | Supply | Gating |
|---|---|---|---|
| SoC 24 MHz | YXC X322524MOB4SI crystal (line 15) | SoC oscillator | SoC |
| RTC 32.768 kHz | Seiko SC-32S crystal on RK809 (line 16) | RK809 | always on |
| DAC MCLK 22.5792 MHz | NSC5083D-45.158M at 45.1584 MHz (line 20), divided by two by one SN74AUC1G74 per DAC (line 139), [ADR 0019](../adr/0019-master-clock-divided-from-twice-the-frequency.md) | Oscillators: MCLK_3V1 (3.1 V), LDO EN = `AUDIO_LN_EN` AND DAC_DIG_1V8 (line 135, G3). Dividers: MCLK_1V8 (1.8 V), LDO EN = DAC_DIG_1V8 (line 29) | ST (`MCLK441_EN`, 3.3 V ball) driven to VIH ≥ 0.7 × 3.1 V = 2.17 V; pull-down on each ST pin so both outputs are tri-stated from power-up (an open ST enables the output). `MCLK_DIV_RUN` on both CLR̅ (100 kΩ pull-down; low = dividers held) |
| DAC MCLK 24.576 MHz | NSC5083D-49.152M at 49.152 MHz (line 21), divided by two (line 139) | as above | `MCLK48_EN`; the two oscillator outputs share one net (33 Ω each, 100 kΩ pull-down at the dividers), one enabled at a time, never both. Start (driver, ADR 0019 decision 6): both ST low and CLR̅ low before the rails rise; release CLR̅ while the net is held low, then raise the wanted ST. Family change: DACs to their internal RCO, CLR̅ low, swap ST, repeat. NDK catalogues phase noise at 49.152 MHz (−158 dBc/Hz at 1 kHz); the 45.1584 MHz figure is **UNVERIFIED** |
| RP2350 12 MHz | ECS ECS-2016MV-120-BN-TR oscillator into XIN (line 14) | RP_IOVDD_1V8 | with RP2354A power |
| PCIe 100 MHz REFCLK | SoC combphy2 internal output | — | v1.13: no consumer (RFC 0014) |

MCLK acceptance criteria for any candidate or second source oscillator (v1.9, [ADR 0019](../adr/0019-master-clock-divided-from-twice-the-frequency.md); before: ADR 0010): 45.1584 / 49.152 MHz, CMOS output, tri-state standby, rated for ≥ 15 pF so the 13.7 pF net fits, phase noise at least as good as the NSC5083D at 49.152 MHz (−158 dBc/Hz at 1 kHz), active, available from authorized channels. Symmetry no longer matters: the divider sets the duty at the DAC. The passive attenuator of ADR 0010 is removed; the XTI level comes from the 1.8 V divider supply.

## 5. Passives and magnetics (estimate)

Counts are scaled from the Radxa ZERO 3W v1.12 schematic (RK3566 + RK817 + LPDDR4 + eMMC + Wi-Fi + 2× USB-C + HDMI: **214 C, 117 R, 16 L, 6 ferrite beads**), plus each datasheet's application circuit for the T1-specific blocks. Values follow the cited application circuits; final counts come from the schematic.

| SoC, RK809, TPS62869 (v1.12; RK860 before), LPDDR4X, eMMC | ~150 | ~40 | 7 | 4 | 100 nF ×~90 (one per supply ball group), 1 µF ×~30, 22 µF ×~25 (buck outputs: 33 µF/22 µF per RK809 app circuit), 4.7 µF ×~12; vcc_ddr 2×22 µF + 1 µF + 10×100 nF (Radxa). v1.12: vdd_cpu converter passives on line 147 (7 C, 5 R with 2 DNP; ADR 0021) and 22 µF + 2 × 4.7 µF under the SoC; LPDDR4X one ZQ resistor, 240 Ω 1 % at ball A5 (ball A8 unfitted, single rank, ADR 0022) |
| Charger, PD, gauge, eFuses, mux, TVS, pre-regulator, 5.1 V buck (v1.5) | ~50 | ~36 | 3 | 0 | BQ25798 (line 115): VBUS 2 × 10 µF, PMID 3 × 10 µF, SYS 5 × 10 µF, BAT 2 × 10 µF, REGN 4.7 µF, BTST 2 × 47 nF, BATP 100 Ω, PROG and ILIM_HIZ resistors; charger inductor 1 µH (line 40). TPS62136: 1.5 µH, CIN 10 µF, COUT 2 × 22 µF, R1/R2 442 kΩ / 100 kΩ. TPS62903 (5.1 V): 1 µH, CIN 10 µF, COUT 22 µF, VSET 95.3 kΩ, S-CONF 26.1 kΩ. TPS2121 OV1 1.02 MΩ / 100 kΩ; eFuse RILM 2.21 kΩ/3.32 kΩ, CdVdt 1.8 nF; gauge 3 mΩ (v1.8, ADR 0017; 5 mΩ before) |
| Audio (2 DACs, 6 OPA1622, rails, clocks; v1.6: + LMR33630C, + 2 LDOs, − TPS65131 inverter) | ~112 | ~98 | 3 | 4 | CS43198: FILT± 15 µF, flying caps 2.2 µF ×4 per DAC (DS1156F2 Fig. 2-1); v1.3 (ADR 0012): 12 difference amplifiers, 1.00 kΩ / 1.62 kΩ 0.1 % thin film ×48 and 100 pF C0G ×24, 1.0 Ω ballast ×12 (lines 37, 107, 108); TPS65131 4.7 µH ×2, 4 × 4.7 µF 25 V X7R per output, dividers 475 k / 100 k and 576 k / 100 k (line 111); LT3045 / LT3094 RSET 60.4 kΩ ×2, CSET 0.47 µF ×2, COUT 10 µF ×2, CIN 4.7 / 10 µF, RILIM 374 Ω / 9.31 kΩ (lines 109–110); XO bypass 10 nF (NDK); ST pull-downs 100 kΩ ×2; v1.9 (ADR 0019, line 140): 33 Ω ×2, 27 Ω ×2, 100 kΩ ×2, divider and MCLK_1V8 LDO decoupling |

| Display, backlight, faceplate FPC | ~12 | ~10 | 1 | 1 | RSET 3.32 Ω (v1.11, TPS61160; 3.40 Ω with the TPS61169), COUT 1 µF 50 V, CIN 4.7 µF |

## 7. Open questions

Closed by the cross-validation: BRD-023 frequencies (confirmed), MaskROM entry (ADR 0009, lines 97–98), battery connector (2-pin JST PH, ADR 0007; v1.5: Micro-Fit 3.0, ADR 0014; v1.10: six spring contacts, ADR 0020), HAT flat-cable connector (line 66), HAT ESD location (pogo field), backlight driver (board).

1. **MCLK (lines 20–21):** the passive attenuator into the 1.8 V XTI input must be confirmed by SPICE before layout freeze (ADR 0010). **Narrowed 2026-10-24** by the SPICE study [`sim/mclk-attenuator/`](../../sim/mclk-attenuator/README.md) (regression in `odapkit sim`): a compensated attenuator, R1 820 Ω with C1 27 pF C0G at each oscillator, 47 Ω in series and 2.0 kΩ with 2.2 pF C0G to ground at each XTI pin (proposed values), meets every XTI limit of DS1156F2 (high ≥ 1.413 V against VIH 1.358 V, peak ≤ 1.900 V against VL + 0.3 = 1.96 V, edges ≤ 2.1 ns, duty 45.6–51.9 % against 45–55 %, no re-crossing) at 22.5792 and 24.576 MHz, MCLK_3V1 3.054–3.147 V, VL 1.66–1.94 V, XTI 1–10 pF and clock branches up to 20 mm, **with a typical oscillator output (30 Ω, UNVERIFIED)**. Open: (a) with the weakest drive the NDK catalogue allows (Tr 6 ns into 15 pF), two XTI pins at their 10 pF maximum load the oscillator beyond its 15 pF rating and no passive values pass: NDK output drive data, the typical XTI capacitance from Cirrus Logic, or a measurement on the first boards decides; (b) the NZ2520SDA symmetry is 40–60 % (catalogue 1703A), wider than the CS43198's 45–55 % (§4.5.1 p. 35): NDK confirmation or screening; (c) layout keeps each clock branch ≤ 20 mm (30 mm already gives 44.8 % duty and 1.97 V peak). **v1.9: closed by [ADR 0019](../adr/0019-master-clock-divided-from-twice-the-frequency.md):** the oscillator runs at twice the frequency and one SN74AUC1G74 per DAC divides it (lines 20, 21, 139), so (a) the oscillator net carries 13.7 pF of its 15 pF and (b) the duty at XTI is set by the divider (45.08–54.35 % simulated, `sim/mclk-divider`); the attenuator is not used. Still **UNVERIFIED** until the first boards: the AUC1G74 additive phase noise and supply sensitivity, the NSC5083D phase noise at 45.1584 MHz and at 3.1 V, the 45.1584 MHz value on an NDK document, whether start-up runts can start the two dividers in opposite phase, the typical XTI capacitance. Layout: oscillator-to-divider ≤ 10 mm, each divider-to-XTI branch ≤ 15 mm.
2. **3.5 mm output topology:** closed in v1.3 by ADR 0012: paralleled halves on every package, six OPA1622.
3. **HAT power on battery (BRD-050, v1.2):** [ADR 0011](../adr/0011-battery-path-rated-for-the-full-output-budget.md) (Proposed when this item was written; later Accepted, then superseded by ADR 0014; updated 2026-10-24) sizes the board for the full budget (4.8 A from the battery) and sets the pack criteria, but no off-the-shelf pack meets them, so the interim Adafruit 328 keeps ≈ 0.4 A at 5.1 V on battery. Maintainers: accept ADR 0011 and its definition of the full budget (1.5 A at 5.1 V shared by HAT_5V and USB-C, plus HAT_3V3 1.0 A; both 5 V ports at 1.5 A together would need ≈ 7.3 A, above the BQ25895's 6 A), and choose the fallback if no pack is found (lower on-battery budget, a made-to-order pack against T1-038, or another charger and cell format). **v1.5: closed by [ADR 0014](../adr/0014-two-cell-battery.md)** (2S pack, a T1-038 exception: the full budget draws ≈ 2.4 A at VSYS 6.6 V). Open: two independent pack makers (line 46).
4. **Item tracking with the T1 off (BRD-030, line 103):** required, or only while the T1 is on or suspended? v1.9: tracking in suspend is decided (2026-10-24, line 103: the radio kept powered while the listener enables it); tracking with the T1 off stays open.
5. **Action button (line 76):** sample deeper-travel switches with the case plunger.
6. **DF40 at 5 Gb/s (line 66):** confirm the Hirose rating and build a test coupon before freeze.
7. **Shielding cans:** fit by default, or only after EMC and audio measurements?
8. **RTC across battery swaps:** accept that the RK809 RTC loses time when the battery is removed?
9. **FPC as a fabricated part (line 102):** confirm that impedance-controlled FPCs count like the PCB under T1-038.
10. **MCLK supply at 3.1 V (line 29, v1.1):** ADR 0010 amended to the TPS7A2031; the phase-noise check at 3.1 V stays for the first boards. v1.9: now at 45.1584 and 49.152 MHz before the dividers (ADR 0019).
11. **Status LED (lines 79, 105, v1.2):** RGB through the LP5521 replaces the yellow-green part. It uses 2 of the 7 faceplate spares (5 left, 9.8 %): accept, or move to the 61-contact FH35C if the ≈ 10 % spare rule must hold strictly (line 66).
12. **HAT reserved contacts (line 66, v1.2):** closed: CAM_PWDN and the DOUT on the DAC clock are dropped, giving 2 reserved contacts on the 100-pin and 5 on the new 50-pin power connector (HAT interface v0).
13. **HP-amp LDO margin (lines 25–26, v1.2):** closed in v1.3 by ADR 0012: LT3045/LT3094 at ±6 V, 275 mA worst continuous = 55 % of 500 mA (D30).
14. **DF40 power connector (line 66, v1.2):** the 30 → 50 contact change touches the flat cable, the device-side piece and their 3D models.
15. **TPS65131 inverter margin (line 22, v1.3, ADR 0012 condition):** 72–79 % of the minimum switch limit by Eq. 4; simulate (WEBENCH or SPICE) at VIN 3.0 V, ±7 V, 275 mA per rail before layout freeze, or replace the inverter (power tree §4.10.1). **Updated 2026-10-24: superseded by [ADR 0015](../adr/0015-amplifier-rails-from-vsys.md)**: the simulation was run ([output-stage simulation](../../sim/output-stage/README.md)), the inverter half is not used and −7 V comes from the LMR33630C (item 26, line 116); the remaining TPS65131 check is item 29.
16. **Paralleled OPA1622 halves (lines 19, 107, v1.3):** current sharing, stability with 1.0 Ω ballast and 400 pF loads, THD+N at 16 and 32 Ω, and the DAC loading with both outputs' networks connected: simulate, then measure.
17. **Full headphone output with an ADR 0011 pack (v1.3):** the ADR 0011 budget (15.9 W from VSYS) has no headphone term beyond the 3.0 W system estimate; full-scale balanced 32 Ω adds ≈ 4.8 W. Whether Blackbird OS budgets headphones and HAT power together, or the budget grows, is for the maintainers (power tree §4.11). v1.5 (2S): the full output budget is 15.7 W from VSYS, ≈ 2.4 A at 6.6 V; adding the full-scale balanced 32 Ω sine (≈ 5.2 W from VSYS through the pre-regulator) gives ≈ 20.9 W, **≈ 3.2 A**: 53 % of the BQ25798 rating, 63 % of the Micro-Fit contact, but 79 % of a 4 A fuse and above a ≥ 3.0 A pack criterion. **Resolved (v1.5, written into ADR 0014):** the battery path covers this combined worst case: pack ≥ 4.0 A continuous (80 %), PCM trip 4.0–5.0 A, lead and plug ≥ 4.6 A per pole (70 %), 5 A board fuse 0451005.MRL (64 %), Micro-Fit contact 64 %, BQ25798 53 %, gauge sense 51 mW (power tree §4.7, §4.10).
18. **Closed 2026-10-25 (RFC 0014): no HAT audio port.** **HAT audio on the DAC clock (lines 95, 112, v1.4):** the first prototype must show that I2S1 RX runs from the DAC's clocks together with the DAC path (hat-standard ADR 0002 item 6). DNP 0 Ω links from the DAC clock nets to SCLKRX/LRCKRX (4_A6/4_A7) keep the fallback without a respin (pin plan §4.3). The DAC clock master must run while a HAT plays or captures.
19. **Closed 2026-10-25 (RFC 0014): no HAT MCLK tap.** **HAT MCLK tap (line 112, v1.4):** a copy of the DAC master clock for the HAT touches the audio clock path (ADR 0010). Tap point and buffer need the maintainers' confirmation and a jitter check on the DAC output before layout freeze. v1.9 (ADR 0019): the oscillator net has 1.3 pF left, so the HAT copy needs its own divider or a buffer behind one, at 3.3 V, and a re-run of `sim/mclk-divider`; still Open (line 112).
20. **Pogo pitch (line 67, v1.4, D36):** the P70-1000045R needs about 2.5 mm pitch; 1.27 mm was assumed earlier. Qualify a pin and fix the pitch (hat-standard interface v1 §10). **Updated 2026-10-24: closed by hat-standard [ADR 0004](https://github.com/opendap-project/hat-standard/blob/main/docs/adr/0004-pogo-part-and-field-geometry.md)** (Accepted): a 4 × 25 field at 2.54 mm along the body's length, Harwin P70 primary, Mill-Max 0965 second source on the same pads (line 67, D36). Its signal-integrity coupon and mating-cycle tests stay required before the pad map freezes.
21. **BQ25798 driver (line 39, v1.5, ADR 0014):** confirm the register map against the mainline BQ25792 support, send the ID upstream, and ship it through DKMS until Debian enables `MFD_BQ257XX`. **Updated 2026-10-24:** the register map matches for everything the driver touches, and the binding (`"ti,bq25798", "ti,bq25792"`) is already in linux-next from another contributor, so nothing is left to send for the ID (ADR 0014 item 5). Open: DKMS until Debian enables `MFD_BQ257XX`; leave the charger's OTG output out of the device tree (ADR 0014 consequences).
22. **BQ28Z610-R1 as a gauge only (line 42, v1.5):** the gauge-only data-flash configuration, the PACK and PBI connections without protection FETs, the I2C address, and Debian's `BATTERY_BQ27XXX_I2C` on arm64 need TI's technical reference manual and a config check before schematic capture. Updated 2026-10-24: `BATTERY_BQ27XXX_I2C` is not set on Debian arm64, so the gauge driver ships through DKMS, and `bq27xxx` binds `ti,bq28z610` (ADR 0014 item 5). **Narrowed 2026-10-24** (TI SLUUC81, January 2020, read): gauge-only data flash set (GAUGE_EN on, FET_EN, PF_EN, BCAST, balancing and firmware protections off), address 0x55, CHG/DSG open, PBI 2.2 µF, the mainline driver only reads the gauge (never writes data flash), DT `compatible = "ti,bq28z610"`, no interrupt. Open: (a) PACK tied to BAT+ without FETs is not described by TI (shutdown never completes above 3.0 V, ≈ 0.2 mA in the PACK pull-down): confirm with TI; (b) 5 mΩ is outside the datasheet's 1–3 mΩ (line 43): TI confirmation or 3 mΩ; (c) TRM default conflicts (Enabled Protections C, AFE Protection Control, IT Gauging Configuration) read back from a first device; (d) CC Gain calibration and the golden image per pack in production. **v1.8: (a), (b) and (c) closed by [ADR 0017](../adr/0017-fuel-gauge-wiring-sense-resistor-and-configuration.md)** (PACK on the switched side of the ship FET with the storage shutdown sequence; 3 mΩ WSL12063L000FEA; the three fields written explicitly after a read-back check). (d) stays open, with the first-module checks of ADR 0017 item 6 (storage drain ≤ 5 µA at the pack, SHUTDOWN and wake, RPACK(PD), read-back values).
23. **Closed in v1.10 by ADR 0020** (the Micro-Fit 3.0 is no longer used; line 47 is six contacts whose drawing is retrieved). Was: **Battery connector footprint (line 47, v1.5):** the Molex 43650-0400 drawing, its per-contact rating with 4 circuits and the mated envelope at the pocket edge were not retrieved; confirm before the case and board outline freeze.
24. **TPS62903 5.1 V thermal (line 91, v1.5):** ≈ 0.6 W at 8.16 W out (est.) in a 1.5 × 2 mm package; check in the board thermal model.
25. **Pack NTC (lines 39, 47, 48, v1.5):** the pack specification must fix the NTC type for the BQ25798 TS window (TI recommends a 103AT-2 10 kΩ, SLUSDV2C Table 5-1).
26. **TPS65131 inverter at full-scale peaks (line 22, v1.5):** the [output-stage simulation](../../sim/output-stage/README.md) found that the TPS65131 −7 V inverter fails at full-scale peaks; a replacement −7 V inverter from VSYS is being chosen. Until that lands, the −7 V rail, line 22 and its margin figures in this BOM stay as they are and are **not** a valid basis for layout. **v1.6: closed by [ADR 0015](../adr/0015-amplifier-rails-from-vsys.md)**: −7 V from an LMR33630C inverting buck-boost on VSYS (line 116), TPS65131 boost only, paired LDOs.
27. **TPS62903 phase margin at low VSYS (line 91, v1.6):** the generic model, corrected against TI's TPS62136 model, gives ≈ 38–40° at VSYS 6.0 V ([VSYS buck and MaskROM simulations](../../sim/README.md)): **UNVERIFIED** until TI's SIMPLIS model (SLVMDP3) or a bench Bode plot at VSYS 6.0 V, 1.6 A. Two 22 µF remain an option after that measurement.
28. **5.1 V output capacitor (line 121, v1.6):** confirm ≥ 12 µF effective at 5.1 V on the maker's DC-bias curve before schematic capture. **Closed 2026-10-24:** GRM32ER71E226KE15L keeps 15.4 µF (AC 0.01 Vrms) to 18.8 µF (AC 0.5 Vrms) at 5.1 V on Murata SimSurfing data (typical, 25 °C; 13.9 µF at −10 % tolerance); CL32B226KAJNNNE ≈ 17.3 µF on Samsung's data. One capacitor stays; temperature and ageing are not in either curve. TDK data not reachable (HTTP 403).
29. **TPS65131 single-output use (line 22, v1.6):** the unused-inverter connection is not in SLVS493E; confirm with TI (E2E) before layout, and measure the boost at the positive LDOs' maximum trip (≈ 99 % of its minimum switch limit, worst-case parameters).
30. **LMR33630C on the bench (line 116, v1.6):** idle PFM bursts at the amplifier output, heat (0.4–0.9 W est. at 0.78 A), and the paired LDOs' current sharing (ADR 0015 consequences).
31. **VSYS_LV ripple in power save (line 113, v1.6):** 15–22 mVpp with load-dependent bursts reach the DAC_VP switch unregulated ([VSYS buck and MaskROM simulations](../../sim/README.md)); the CS43198 VP rejection is **UNVERIFIED**; forced PWM is the fallback (bench item).

32. **Radio supplies (lines 55, 129–131, v1.8):** the Type 2EL datasheet (supply ranges, AVDD33/AVDD18/VIO sequencing, module-level peaks) was not obtained (Murata answered 403); the AVDD18 rail is sized from the hardware application note and the carrier's peak. Get the datasheet, then confirm EN-from-SWOUT1 or fit the GPIO option.
33. **LPDDR4X supplies (lines 8, 9, 132, v1.8):** v1.12 (ADR 0022): the windows are now public for the Micron part, VDD1 1.70–1.95 V, VDD2 1.06–1.17 V, VDDQ 0.57–0.65 V (z42n Rev. C), and every board rail sits inside them (power tree v0.6 §4.8); the IDD tables are public too (z42n pp. 50–57) but not yet transcribed into the VDD1 and VDDQ budgets, so the currents stay open. Before v1.12: VDD1 and VDD2 currents and windows need the Samsung (or SK hynix) datasheet; the 100 mA VDD1 allowance is an estimate. The TPS7A1006 runs at ≈ 0.48 V headroom at the vcc_ddr low corner (1.083 V) against the 0.5 V test condition of SBVS314B: confirm with TI, or set vcc_ddr nearer 1.125 V (R1 48.7 kΩ / 120 kΩ) if the memory's VDD2 window allows.
34. **CS43198 control pins unpowered (line 133, v1.8):** fail-safe behaviour decides whether the isolation switch or its bypass is fitted in `production`. The I2C2 pull-up value (2.2 or 4.7 kΩ) is chosen with the switch's 120 Ω maximum on-resistance and the bus rise time.
35. **vcca1v8_pmu load (power tree §4.13, v1.8):** ≈ 160 mA (est.) against RK809 LDO8's 400 mA (VIN 3.6 V figure) or 200 mA (VIN 2 V figure); the 3.3 V-input rating is not tabulated. Measure on the first boards with the line 137 links; the line 132 LDO takes VDD1 off it if needed.
36. **vcc3v3_sys peak (line 6, v1.8):** the module's AVDD33 peak (1.05 A instead of the 0.6 A estimate) raises the estimated peak to ≈ 2.35 A = 78 % of the TPS62826's 3 A. Re-sum from the schematic; the pin-compatible TPS62827 (4 A, 59 %) is the fit if the sum passes 80 %. **v1.9: closed:** the re-sum gives ≈ 2.45 A (82 %), so line 6 is the TPS62827 (61 %).
37. **Pinout v1 (v1.8):** IO-domain assumptions to reconcile: radio VIO/SD_VIO on vcc_1v8 (VCCIO4); TPM reset and PIRQ, PN7160 VEN and DWL_REQ, NFC tag FD on native 1.8 V GPIO; RP2354A taps on FT GPIO0–25; `HAPTIC_EN` and `NFC_PWR_EN` on expander spares; SoC PERST# on VCCIO1 (3.3 V). **v1.9: closed by [pinout v1](pinout-v1.md) §9.1:** every assumption holds except `HAT3V3_EN`, which moves to expander P01 (line 38).
38. **2 GB LPDDR4X fallback and SK hynix second source (line 8, G18):** land and rkbin support unchecked; not addressed in v1.8 or v1.9. v1.12 (ADR 0022): the 4 GB second source is now CXMT CXDB5CBAM-MA-B, with its land checked by kicad-lib (`test_bga.py` compares its ballout with Micron's ball for ball) and its blob support on Rockchip's list (Ver 2.65 p. 11): closed for 4 GB; SK hynix removed. The 2 GB fallback stays open and unnamed (ADR 0013).
39. **Closed 2026-10-24:** `MCLK_DIV_RUN` on GPIO4_C4 with a 100 kΩ pull-down (pinout v1 §3). Was: **`MCLK_DIV_RUN` ball (line 139, v1.9):** ADR 0019 asks for an expander line or a GPIO with a 100 kΩ pull-down; pinout v1 has no ball for it. Proposal for the pinout owner: spare GPIO4_C4 (`SPARE_4C4`, VCCIO7, `_d`, so the dividers are held at reset), next to the ST lines on the same bank; the AUC inputs accept 3.6 V at any VCC (SCES537D p. 3). Expander P07 is the alternative.
40. **Closed 2026-10-24:** `WL_PDN_HOLD` on expander P07 through an SN74AXC1T45 to `vcc1v8_wl` (add one SN74AXC1T45DCKR to the translator line), `WL_PWR_EN` on P06; GPIO2_B7 spare (pinout v1 §3). Was: **Radio kept powered in suspend (lines 50, 55, 103, v1.9):** (a) `WL_PDN` sits on GPIO2_B7 (VCCIO4), which loses its state in Rockchip's standby (HDG p. 35), so with tracking enabled it needs a line that holds through suspend; pinout v1 has no free PMU ball and the expander's ports are 3.3 V against the module's 1.8 V pin: a choice for the pinout owner (for example an open-drain stage from expander P07, or a pull-up to the module VIO with the SoC pad released). (b) Module PDn level, wake polarities and the AVDD33/AVDD18/VIO order wait for the Type 2EL datasheet (pinout O12). (c) The suspend draw with tracking on, measured on M-RF through links 11 and 12. `WL_PWR_EN` on expander P06 is to be recorded in pinout v1 §3.
41. **Pinout v1 values (line 141, v1.9):** the VCCIO6 ferrite bead (only if ripple asks for it), the `USB_OTG0_VBUSDET` divider and the pin's rating against VBUS up to 12.6 V, and the `HAT_AUD_OE_N` pull-up value are **UNVERIFIED** until the Rockchip reference schematic or the bench.
42. **Battery contacts (lines 46, 47, 142, 143, v1.10, ADR 0020):** before the board freezes, obtain KYOCERA AVX product specification 201-01-252 (contact force, resistance, current-rating basis) and replace the assumed 20 mΩ end-of-life resistance and the 1.25 sharing factor with it and CB-01; the case tolerance stack that holds 2.1 ± 0.3 mm, the contact window and liner, and the back cover's foam preload above six contact forces (case inputs); the cover-detect actuator (case; the GPIO is GPIO0_A6, pinout v1 §7); whether the P+ TVS is fitted (CB-07, CB-10); the pack makers' PCM short-circuit delay. The pack-size arithmetic no longer needs the 2.5 mm lead-exit allowance: about +4 % of pack length (est.) for the pack or the case, a decision for the pack specification and the case. Bring-up uses a bench terminal board with the pack's pad layout, so prototype 1 runs through the same contacts (ADR 0020 item 9).
43. **vdd_cpu on the first boards (lines 3, 4, 147, v1.12, ADR 0021):** the C option regulates from VOUT Register 1 (write it, measure vdd_cpu; if not, fit the TPS628691ARQYR and set `reg` to 0x41); load step at the SoC (0 → 3 A estimate) with the remote sense and the H5 link, ripple ≤ 60 mV (HDG 1.1 p. 36), else the 0 Ω local-sense option or the TPS6287x; vdd_cpu ready before the RK809-5 releases RESETn; the RK3566 vdd_cpu peak under all-core load on the H5 link; suspend draw with vdd_cpu on; efficiency at 3.8 V and 0.15 A; the input capacitors' effective value at 3.8 V (≥ 8 µF).
44. **LPDDR4X (line 8, v1.12, ADR 0022):** the grade (IT:C, WT:C or AAT:C after its package check); a formal lifecycle statement from Micron for the `:C` grades and from CXMT; the effect of a single rank on the experience budgets (T1-046, T1-047), measured at bring-up.
45. **DF40 contact count (lines 66, 102, v1.12; v1.14: the DF40C-50, 46 usable positions by catalog note 3, so only the numbering stays open):** 96 or 100 usable contacts (catalog note against kicad-lib's reading of drawing EDC3-311351-00) and the numbering, from Hirose's drawing for approval; the allocation on positions 3–98 does not depend on it.
46. **Closed 2026-10-25 (v1.14, RFC 0015): eFuse #D, a shared limiter at 2.520 A maximum, keeps the buck at 84 % of its rating (line 44).** Was: **5.1 V buck in a double eFuse fault (line 91, v1.13):** both eFuses at their 1.981 A maximum trip reach 3.96 A, 132 % of the buck's 3 A rating; software keeps the sum at the 1.6 A design. Thermal check in the board model (power tree v0.7 §8 item 29).
47. **HAT `VBUS` discharge (lines 35, 44, v1.13):** the series resistor for the discharge FET, from the port's capacitance and the USB PD discharge time; and whether the TPS259470 output needs it on the main USB-C as well.
48. **Closed 2026-10-25 (v1.14, RFC 0015): 5 V only.** The HAT input path is rated for 5 V (TVS0500 5 V stand-off, FUSB302B `CC` 6.0 V absolute maximum); OV2 at 5.60–6.31 V (line 45). Accepting more is a later board revision. Was: **PD voltages the T1 accepts from a HAT (lines 45, 65, 69, v1.13, RFC 0014):** 5 V until the maintainers decide. Above 5 V: the TPS2121 OV2 threshold, a `VBUS` TVS rated for it, and `CC` protection against a short to `VBUS` (FUSB302B `CC` absolute maximum 6.0 V, FUSB302B/D Rev. 5 p. 14).
49. **USB3 HOST1 remote wakeup from deep suspend (v1.13, RFC 0014):** the HAT's wake path; **UNVERIFIED** on the RK3566.
50. **Closed 2026-10-25 (v1.14, RFC 0015): the pogo field** (lines 66, 67, 102; ESD on the piece, lines 65, 68, 69). Was: **HAT connector (lines 66, 67, 102, v1.13):** the pogo field on the device-side piece or a USB-C receptacle on the HAT case side, reached by the flat cable. The connector part, the pogo count, the cable's conductors and its spec follow the decision; the ESD and TVS positions (lines 65, 68, 69) follow it too.
51. **VSYS converter input capacitance (C901, C4402; v1.17):** one 10 µF 25 V X5R 0805 keeps 1.86 µF effective at 8.8 V and −20 °C (2.11 µF at 25 °C, tolerance included; Murata SimSurfing) against the 3 µF effective minimum of the TPS62136 (SLVSDV2 §7.3) and the TPS62903 (SLVSES3A §6.3). A second capacitor per converter, or a part that keeps 3 µF at 8.8 V, before the board freezes.
52. **GRM155R61A105KE15D (1 µF 10 V X5R 0402, v1.17):** Murata's product data lists it as to be discontinued (SimSurfing product list, 2026-10-27). Every position that uses it needs a successor with the same or better DC-bias figure.
53. **GRM21BR71C106KE51L (10 µF 16 V X7R 0805, amplifier-rail LDOs, v1.17):** not in Murata's product data (SimSurfing product list, 2026-10-27): no DC-bias curve or lifecycle could be read. Confirm the part number and its effective capacitance at ±6.04 V and −7.06 V against the LT3045 and LT3094 10 µF minimums.

## 8. Risks

1. **MCLK** (lines 20, 21, 139): attenuator simulated (2026-10-24); passes with a typical oscillator output, not with the catalogue's weakest drive, and the oscillator's 40–60 % symmetry exceeds the DAC's 45–55 %: see §7 item 1 and ADR 0010. v1.9: replaced by the divide-by-two of ADR 0019; the remaining risk is the divider's unpublished additive phase noise and supply sensitivity (estimates leave 43 dB under the CS43198 mask at 100 kHz) and opposite-phase divider start. Fallback (ADR 0019 decision 7): Kyocera KC2520K at 1.8 V with a buffer per DAC, at the cost of the duty guarantee and about 20 dB of close-in phase noise.
2. **Memory lifecycle** (lines 8, 10): v1.12 (ADR 0022, read 2026-10-25): the Samsung LPDDR4X is discontinued and Samsung and Micron have left consumer LPDDR4/LPDDR4X; the Micron MT53E1G32D2FW-046 IT:C and WT:C grades are orderable through authorized channels, not held in distributor stock; the CXMT second source has no franchised distributor. Line 10: one eMMC brand with an authorized source.
3. **DAC sourcing** (line 18): one authorized source, for a part needed twice per unit.
4. **Display sourcing** (line 81): no production channel confirmed yet.
5. **Battery current limit** (lines 46, 47, 104): 1.6 A (derated, v1.1) through the JST PH contact on 26 AWG leads; peak loads are budgeted in software and, from v1.2, bounded by a 2.5 A fuse. v1.5: superseded by ADR 0014: ≈ 3.2 A worst case through a Micro-Fit 3.0 connector (64 %) and a 5 A fuse (64 %); the risk moves to the pack (risk 16). v1.10: six spring contacts (ADR 0020), 33 % on the hotter contact; the risk moves to contact pressure and resistance stability (risk 25).
6. **DKMS drivers** until Debian enables them (lines 18, 39, 42, 55, 81; the display driver ships from v7.3). v1.5: the BQ25798 binding is in linux-next, not yet in master (line 39, checked 2026-10-24); the BQ28Z610-R1 needs only `BATTERY_BQ27XXX_I2C` (line 42).
7. **Thin supply:** TPD8S300A, INA236, SN74AXC4T774PWR, SN74AXC1T45, SN74LVC1G57DCKR, LP5521YQX, ECS-2016MV-120, Murata Type 2EL, v1.3: LT3045EDD#TRPBF, OPA1622 at six per board, Würth 744373240047, v1.5: BQ28Z610DRZR-R1, TPS62136RGXR, TPS62903RPJR at two per board, Coilcraft XGL4020-102MEC at two per board and XGL4020-152MEC, Molex 43650-0400. The Sourcing column of §2 records which have one authorized source or none.
8. **RK809-5 OTP defaults** are not public (line 2); a wrong map is a hardware respin. The VCC feeds are now copied from the ROCK 3C v1.4 (RK809-5), but feeding BUCK1–4 from VSYS follows the datasheet application circuit, not a shipping RK809-5 board. ADR 0009 cites Quartz64 and Radxa ZERO as RK809 references, but both use the RK817; the RK809-5 references are the ROCK 3C and Orange Pi 3B.
9. **TPS65131 current at low battery** (line 22): v1.3 needs 275 mA per rail at ±7 V; the inverter sits at 79 % of its minimum switch limit at VIN 3.0 V (Eq. 4) and the typical curve gives ≈ 340 mA there. Simulation before layout freeze (ADR 0012). v1.5: the TPS65131 runs from the regulated 3.8 V VSYS_LV (line 113), so the inverter sits at 68 % (Eq. 4 at 3.8 V). The output-stage simulation shows it failing at full-scale peaks anyway (§7 item 26). **Updated 2026-10-24: superseded by [ADR 0015](../adr/0015-amplifier-rails-from-vsys.md)**: the inverter half is not used (−7 V from the LMR33630C, line 116); the remaining TPS65131 boost item is §7 item 29.
10. **VBUS surge clamp above downstream absolute maxima** (line 64).
11. **5 Gb/s over FPC + DF40 + pogo** unproven (line 66).
12. **MaskROM combo timing** against the RK3566 BootROM is unverified (lines 97–98).
13. **Clone parts** (TPD4E05U06, TVS2200, TPS7A2050 clones exist): production pins genuine-brand parts from authorized channels.
14. **Sourcing data is dated** (2026-10-22 to 2026-10-25) and must be re-checked before each build (T1-029).
15. **vcc3v3_sys peak load** (line 6): closed in v1.2 by the 3 A TPS62826 (62 % at the 1.85 A estimate); re-sum from the schematic, with the 4 A TPS62827 as drop-in. v1.9: the re-sum (2.45 A, est.) passed 80 %, so the TPS62827 is fitted (61 %); the schematic sum still decides.
16. **The pack is made to order** (lines 46, 104, ADR 0014; v1.5, was: no battery meets BRD-050 yet): BRD-050 is met on paper (≈ 2.4 A at the full budget, ≈ 3.2 A worst case with headphones), but no off-the-shelf 2S pack fits the pocket with a PCM; the product depends on at least two independent makers building to the ADR 0014 item 2 specification. Until then, bring-up runs on a bench supply or a protected 2S pack on the bench.
17. **Headphone output on the interim pack** (v1.3, ADR 0012): the full-scale balanced 32 Ω sine alone reaches the 1.6 A battery budget at VSYS 3.0 V; a software output ceiling is required (power tree §4.11). v1.5: closed by ADR 0014 item 3: no interim pack and no output ceiling from the battery; the combined worst case is §7 item 17.
18. **Paralleled OPA1622 halves** are not described by TI (SBOS727B); the 240 / 640 mW figures are desk estimates (lines 19, 107).
19. **BQ25798 software path** (line 39, v1.5; updated 2026-10-24): the binding (`ti,bq25798` with the `ti,bq25792` fallback) is in linux-next but not yet in master, and the mainline-supported BQ25792 has, per the upstream commit, been unpublished by TI. DKMS carries the driver until Debian enables it (ADR 0014 item 5, OS-023).
20. **Gauge without board-side protection** (line 42, v1.5): protection and balancing rely on the pack PCM (ADR 0014 item 2); the board adds the 5 A fuse and the BQ25798 IBAT_OCP (9.3 A min). A pack that fails its PCM is bounded only by the fuse.
21. **Thin supply (v1.6):** TPS3808G01DBVR, XGL4020-222MEC (no authorized source), CSD17575Q3 (authorized channels carry only the 250-piece CSD17575Q3T), LT3045EDD#TRPBF at two per board, LMR33630CRNXR.
22. **Suspend memory retention (v1.8, closed by design):** until v1.8 the LPDDR4X VDD1 and the VDDQ LDO bias sat on vcc_1v8, which the reference device trees turn off in suspend; v1.8 moves both to vcca1v8_pmu (line 132). Bring-up must still confirm self-refresh across suspend.
23. **Thin supply (v1.8):** SN74LVC2G66DCUR, INA236BIDDFR, Würth 744373240047 now on three lines, KLMAG1JETD-B041 (one authorized source), RL0603FR-070R1L.
24. **Thin supply (v1.9):** NSC5083D-45.158M and NSC5083D-49.152M (one authorized source each), SN74AUC1G74DCUR, TPS62827DMQR.
25. **Battery contact interface (v1.10, lines 46, 47; ADR 0020):** contact interfaces fail by loss of contact pressure after drops, and by resistance that drifts with fretting or contamination, which would corrupt the gauge's learned impedance. The contact has no same-land second source, and its force and resistance are in a specification not yet obtained. Mitigation: the back cover's foam, not the springs, holds the pack down; qualification CB-01 to CB-12 with the T1 values ([battery pack specification](battery-pack.md) §2), drop tests with event detection on BAT (CB-04); Mill-Max pogo pins on their own land as the fallback.

## 9. Datasheet review (spec sheets, 2026-10-22)

Each line's pick was checked against its datasheet (2026-10-22). *Resolved* items are decided here; *Open* items must be closed before schematic capture. v1.1 (2026-10-22) re-worked every *Open* item; the status column records the outcome and its evidence. The datasheets are listed in the Datasheets table and in the libs parts manifest.

| # | Lines | Finding (source: the part's datasheet) | Status and action |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | 17 | AT24C02D in SOT23 has no A0–A2 pins: it answers only at 0x50, not 0x57 | Resolved: 0x50 on I2C2 (free; address map in §4 updated) |
| D2 | HPA rails | TPS7A4901 EN needs ≥ 2.1 V and TPS7A3001 EN ≥ 2 V; a 1.8 V GPIO cannot enable them | Resolved: `HPA_RAIL_EN` is driven at 3.3 V logic (TCA6416A port at 3.3 V or a single-bit translator, pinout v1). v1.3: the LT3045/LT3094 EN/UV thresholds are 1.24 V and 1.26 V (max 1.32 / 1.35 V), so the 3.3 V net still drives them |
| D3 | HPA rails | The two LDOs ramp at different rates per nF of soft-start capacitance; equal capacitors do not give a symmetric ±5 V ramp | Resolved (v1.1): CNR/SS 10 nF on the TPS7A4901 (tSS = 1.4 ms/nF → 14 ms, SBVS121E Eq. 1) and 15 nF on the TPS7A3001 (0.9 ms/nF → 13.5 ms, SBVS125D Eq. 1): ramps matched within 4 %, ≤ 0.2 V apart mid-ramp. Both LDO ENs on one 3.3 V net, raised after the TPS65131 pre-rails settle, so the LDO soft-start alone sets the ramp; OPA1622 EN held low throughout (SBOS727B §7.4.2). The equations are typical and the TPS7A30 curve is given without CFF: ramp symmetry is a bring-up measurement (lines 25–26). v1.3: superseded by D30 (LT3045/LT3094 share tSS ≈ 2.3 × RSET × CSET; line 110) |
| D4 | 28 | TPS62840: 2.2 V is a valid setting (RSET 4.32 kΩ); the 1 µH inductor alternative is not allowed | Resolved: 2.2 V confirmed; 1 µH alternative removed |
| D5 | 20–21, 29 | TPS7A2033 has 0.2 V headroom at VSYS 3.5 V; TI recommends ≥ 0.3 V. NDK gives phase noise at 22.5792 MHz only. ST of the idle oscillator needs a defined low from power-up | Resolved (v1.1): MCLK LDO → TPS7A2031PDBVR (same SOT-23-5 land and datasheet SBVS338H, active). 3.1 V ±1.5 % is inside the NSC5083D 3.3 ± 0.33 V supply and keeps ≥ 0.3 V headroom down to VSYS = 3.4 V (VSYS follows VBAT on battery, so the 3.5 V VSYSMIN did not hold). ST pull-downs added (§4.3). Phase noise at 3.1 V and at 24.576 MHz **UNVERIFIED** (NDK gives 22.5792 MHz at 3.3 V only). Amends the supply part named in ADR 0010: maintainers to confirm (§7 item 10) |
| D6 | RP2354A | QSPI flash supply and two other supplies need 3.3 V with I/O at 1.8 V; crystal circuit tuned for 3.3 V I/O; firmware must select 1.8 V I/O | Resolved (v1.1): RP_3V3 feeds VREG_VIN + VREG_AVDD (ramp together), QSPI_IOVDD, USB_OTP_VDD and ADC_AVDD; RP_IOVDD_1V8 feeds IOVDD (DS Table 1688, §6.1.8). Crystal replaced by an ECS-2016MV-120-BN-TR oscillator (1.6–3.6 V, ±50 ppm, ECS-2016MV Rev. 2025) on RP_IOVDD_1V8 into XIN, which accepts a 1–50 MHz square wave (DS Table 1686), so no re-tuning of the 3.3 V crystal circuit is needed. Firmware sets bank-0 VOLTAGE_SELECT = 1 (DS §6.1.1; firmware note for blackbird-os). Lines 12, 14; power tree §3 |
| D7 | 36, 74 | TPD1E10B06 must sit at the connector, not at the OPA1622 feedback node | Resolved: placed at the jack and button pads |
| D8 | 95 | SN74AXC2T45 has no OE pin, so it cannot isolate an absent HAT | Resolved (v1.1): SN74AXC2T245RSWR (DIR1, DIR2, common active-low OE; Ioff; 380 Mb/s 1.8 ↔ 3.3 V; SCES879A), second source SN74AVC2T245RSWR. Line 95 |
| D9 | 94 | TCA9406 is not a pin-compatible second source for the PCA9306; the PCA9306 EN must be at the 3.3 V side | Resolved: TCA9406 removed as second source; EN tied per the datasheet application circuit |
| D10 | 97–98 | TPS3839 delay is 120–350 ms, not 200 ms; SN74LVC1G27 has no specified hysteresis and needs fast edges | Resolved (v1.1) for the logic: window specified as td = 120–350 ms (SBVS193D); the NOR becomes SN74LVC1G57 (NOR) + SN74LVC1G58 (AND with inverted input), both with Schmitt inputs (ΔVT 0.30–0.62 V at 1.65 V). Line 98. The match of 120–350 ms against the RK3566 BootROM eMMC sampling stays **UNVERIFIED** (risk 12) v1.6: the simulation showed the window too short to be guaranteed and reopened at every resume; replaced by a TPS3808G01 on vcca1v8_pmu with a 1.21–3.43 s window (line 97) |
| D11 | 76 | KMR2 travel is 0.25 mm: still fails T1-022 | Open (already open in §7) |
| D12 | 66 | DF40 corner contacts are metal hold-downs: 96 + 26 usable contacts, one fewer than the 97 allocated on the 100-pin | Resolved (v1.1): HAT_DET# moves to the 30-pin power connector (25 of 26 usable); the 100-pin carries 96 of 96 usable contacts. No spare left on the 100-pin (§7 item 12); v1.2 frees 2 reserved contacts and moves power to a 50-contact pair (line 66). 5 Gb/s rating still **UNVERIFIED** (DF40 catalog gives no figure; §7 item 6) |
| D13 | 57 | Molex 146187-0050 is a rigid PCB antenna with a cable, not an FPC antenna | Resolved: description corrected here; part kept v1.8: the part is replaced by the Molex 146153-0050 ([ADR 0018](../adr/0018-antennas-and-certification-route.md)). |
| D14 | 81 | The display has a single DSI lane and an ILI9488 controller; no mainline DRM driver was confirmed; VCI absolute maximum 3.3 V | Resolved (v1.1): mainline has `drivers/gpu/drm/panel/panel-ilitek-ili9488.c` (commit 99e9aca83bfb, first in v7.3, absent in v7.2), a MIPI DSI driver whose only compatible is `focuslcds,e35gh-i-mw800cb`, with binding `ilitek,ili9488.yaml` (requires vci-supply, iovcc-supply, reset-gpios). 1 lane, RGB666, 14.4 MHz pixel clock ≈ 337 Mb/s per lane, inside the RK3566 Inno DSI PHY range; a 1-lane RK3566 board running it is **UNVERIFIED** by example. Driver path: DKMS on Debian 7.2, mainline from 7.3. The E35GH stays; faceplate-touch ADR 0001 unchanged. VCI held at 2.8 V (absolute maximum 3.3 V). |
| D15 | 85 | The display spec names no connector for the 6-pin touch tail; FH12-6S is not backed by the spec | Resolved (v1.1): the spec's outline drawing (rev 1.0, p. 3) gives the CTP tail: 6 contacts, P 0.50 mm, W 0.30 mm, width 3.50 ± 0.1 mm, 0.30 mm thick, i.e. the FH12-6S FPC geometry (3.5 ± 0.07 mm, 0.3 ± 0.05 mm). Contact side and the 0.30 mm conductor width (FH12 recommends 0.35 ± 0.03 mm) to check on a sample (line 85) |
| D16 | 79 | Green LED VF reaches 3.7 V at 20 mA; a 3.3 V drive cannot reach it | Resolved (v1.1): status LED → Everlight 19-217/G7C-AN1P2/6T (AlGaInP yellow-green, VF ≤ 2.4 V at 20 mA, DSE-0014851 Rev 4) with 270 Ω from the 3.3 V PWM pin (3.3–4.8 mA). Colour change for the maintainers to accept (§7 item 11; line 79). Superseded in v1.2 by the RGB LED with the LP5521 (lines 79, 105) |
| D17 | 72 | TPD6E05U06 RVZ package drawing is not in the datasheet | Resolved (v1.1): TPD6E05U06RVZR dropped. TI's datasheet (SLVSBO7O) carries outlines for DYA, DQA and DPY only, and the RVZ outline was not found on ti.com. Lines 69, 73 and 75 use TPD4E05U06DQAR (DQA0010A outline and land pattern in SLVSBO7O): smaller per channel (2.5 mm² for 4 channels against ≈ 6.2 mm² for 6) and one ESD part number for all low-speed lines |
| D18 | 38 (HAT boost) | TPS61088: TI returns 404 for datasheet and product page | Resolved (v1.1): ti.com returns 404 for the TPS61088 product page, part page and datasheet (only TPS61088-Q1 is listed); Replaced by TI TPS61089RNRR (active on ti.com; SLVSD38C): 2.7–12 V in, 4.5–12.6 V out, ILIM = 1,030,000 / RILIM, 1 µA shutdown. Sized for 1.6 A at 5.1 V (RILIM 200 kΩ, ILIM 5.15 A typ; XAL4020-152 kept). Lines 91–92; power tree §4.6 |
| D19 | 2 | The public RK809 datasheet covers the RK809-1 (OTP: BUCK4 3.0 V, BUCK5 3.3 V); the RK809-5 defaults are unknown. SWOUT1 is 1.5 A and SWOUT2 3 A (reversed in the power tree); SWOUT2 is fed from VCC8, and the reference circuit feeds VCC6 and VCC8 from BUCK5; LDO4 is 400 mA or 100 mA depending on the table | Partly resolved (v1.1): power tree §2–§3 re-derived from the Radxa ROCK 3C v1.4 schematic (PMIC marked RK809-5) and three mainline RK3566 + RK809 device trees. SWOUT1 1.5 A (VCC9) → IW612; SWOUT2 3 A (VCC8) → microSD; VCC5–VCC9 on vcc3v3_sys (now always-on, EXT_EN unused); VCC1–VCC4 on VSYS (datasheet application circuit, not a reference board); LDO3 100 mA, other LDOs 400 mA, LDO4 designed to 100 mA (tables disagree). Open: RK809-5 OTP voltages and sequence are not public; request them from Rockchip before the rail map is frozen |
| D20 | 39 | BQ25895: TS thresholds are fixed REGN fractions (0–40 °C only through the NTC divider); watchdog expiry restores 2048 mA; OTG pin must be tied low | Resolved: NTC divider sized for 0–40 °C; OTG tied low; Blackbird OS services the watchdog (requirement to add in blackbird-os) v1.5: the BQ25895 is replaced by the BQ25798 (line 39); its TS window and watchdog behaviour are re-checked with the pack NTC (§7 item 25) |
| D21 | 45 (mux) | TPS2121 RON up to 100 mΩ (0.4 W at 2 A); OV1 threshold differs between BOM (≈ 13.7 V) and power tree (≈ 14.5 V) | Resolved (v1.1): OV1 = 11.9 V typ (1.02 MΩ / 100 kΩ, 11.1–12.6 V worst case), above a 9 V PD contract plus 5 % and below the BQ25895 14 V operating maximum; the same value in BOM and power tree. Worst-case RON 100 mΩ at 2 A = 0.40 W → +29 °C (RθJA 72.2 °C/W, JEDEC board): acceptable; final check in the board thermal model (line 45) |
| D22 | 19 | TPS259470: current-limit accuracy about ±15 % at 1 A; TI recommends a TVS from OUT to IN on the USB-C eFuse | Resolved: TVS added to the USB-C eFuse (part chosen in v1.1); limits set with the tolerance |
| D23 | INA236 | Powers up converting continuously; 100 mΩ shunts cap the audio and display monitors at 0.82 A | Resolved: driver configures it at boot; shunts resized in v1.1 |
| D24 | 46 | JST PH 2 A rating is for AWG #24; the Adafruit 328 lead is 26 AWG, so the 2.0 A battery budget is unconfirmed | Resolved (v1.1) by derating: the ePH catalog rates 2 A for AWG #24 only and gives no 26 AWG figure. Battery path budget 1.6 A continuous (est., equal heating per length: 2 A × √(0.129 / 0.205)), enforced in software on the VSYS INA236; HAT/USB-C source on battery drops to ≈ 0.4 A at 5.1 V (power tree §4.6–4.7). A JST or Adafruit 26 AWG rating would restore 2 A. v1.2: a 2.5 A fuse now bounds this path (line 104); the full budget needs another pack ([ADR 0011](../adr/0011-battery-path-rated-for-the-full-output-budget.md)) |
| D25 | Backlight | TPS61169 open-LED protection up to 39 V leaves 1 V margin on the 40 V PMEG4010CEH; orderable part rated −40 to 85 °C | Resolved (v1.1): PMEG6010CEH,115 (60 V, 1 A, VF ≤ 660 mV at 1 A, same SOD123F land) gives 21 V over the 39 V OVP maximum. Correction: the −40 to 85 °C rating is the TPS61169DCKR's orderable range; the PMEG4010CEH datasheet gives Tj 150 °C (line 53) |
| D26 | 6 | The TPS62827 (4 A alternative) needs at least 20 µF effective output capacitance (SLVSEF9I Table 8-4), so it is not a drop-in on 2 × 10 µF | Resolved: the vcc3v3_sys output uses 22 µF + 10 µF, valid for both TPS62826 and TPS62827. v1.9: the TPS62827 is fitted (line 6); ≥ 20 µF effective at 3.3 V checked on the maker's DC-bias curve at schematic capture |
| D27 | 4, 6, 106 | DFE252012F-R47M is rated 4.9 A (40 °C rise), below the TPS62826's 5.0 A maximum current limit, and it is not on TI's recommended inductor list | Resolved (v1.3): vcc3v3_sys takes the **Würth 744373240047** (line 106), on TI's list (SLVSEF9I Table 8-5: 0.47 µH, 6.8 A, 11.2 mΩ max). Würth product page: IR 8.7 A (ΔT 40 K), Isat 10.4 A at 10 % drop; the 5.0 A maximum limit is 74 % of TI's 6.8 A. Coilcraft XFL4015-471MEC (6.6 A at 30 % drop, 5.4 A at 20 %) is the second source. The RK809 and vdd_cpu bucks keep the DFE252012F-R47M (lines 4–5) |
| D28 | 104 | The 0451 fuse is only guaranteed to open at 200 % of its rating: the 2.5 A part protects against a short, not against a sustained 2–2.5 A overload of the interim pack's 2 A JST PH contact | Resolved: sustained overload is prevented upstream by the battery-current cap (charger and software budget, 1.6 A with the interim pack); the fuse is the short-circuit protection. With an ADR 0011 pack the 7 A part applies. Opening times, cold resistance and 300–400 A interrupting rating at 32 V DC now come from the datasheet v1.5: the 5 A 0451005.MRL (3.2 A worst case = 64 %) replaces both; sustained overload is bounded by the pack PCM (trip 4.0–5.0 A, ADR 0014) |
| D29 | 105 | The LP5521 green/blue headroom (0.29 V) is a typical figure: the 3.5 Ω pump resistance has no maximum, and VSAT 0.1 V is specified at 17.5 mA | Resolved: each channel is capped at 17.5 mA in the device tree (`led-max-microamp = <17500>`), inside the specified VSAT condition; indicator use is 2–5 mA |
| D30 | 25–26, 109–110 | LT3045/LT3094 fit the ±5 V rails electrically (500 mA, 0.8 µVrms) but are not drop-in: DFN-10/12, different passives, LT3094 exposed pad at −6 V, about 4.5 mA more quiescent current | Resolved (v1.3) by ADR 0012: LT3045EDD#TRPBF (+6.04 V) and LT3094EDD#TRPBF (−6.04 V) replace the TPS7A4901/TPS7A3001, which would run at 183 % / 138 % at the 275 mA worst continuous load. From the datasheets (LT3045 Rev. D, LT3094 Rev. B): RSET 60.4 kΩ (VOUT = 100 µA × RSET), 275 mA = 55 % of 500 mA, 1 V headroom against ≤ 350 / 410 mV maximum dropout, PSRR ≈ 65–75 dB near 1.25 MHz at 1 V headroom, GND current 2.2 / 2.35 mA typ (≈ 4.5 mA together at no load), soft-start tSS ≈ 2.3 × RSET × CSET on both (equal RC ramps), LT3094 exposed pad on IN (−7 V). New DFN-10 and DFN-12 footprints; passives on lines 109–110 |
| D31 | 22–23 | DFE252012F-4R7M: 2.1 A rating at ΔL/L −30 % and 1.5 A at ΔT 40 °C (Murata sheet); the TPS65131 switch limit is 1.8 / 1.95 / 2.2 A. At 275 mA per rail and VIN 3.0 V the inverter inductor peaks at 1.43 A (68 %) with ≈ 1.15 A average (76 %, est.); an overload reaches the 2.2 A maximum limit, above the 2.1 A rating | Open (v1.3): keep the part while the simulation of ADR 0012 runs, or move to a 4.7 µH inductor rated above 2.2 A from a series on TI's list (SLVS493E Table 8-3: EPCOS B8246284-G4, Würth 7447789XXX / 744031XXX, TDK VLF3010 / VLF4012); Updated 2026-10-24: [ADR 0015](../adr/0015-amplifier-rails-from-vsys.md) leaves the inverter half and its inductor unused (line 23: qty 2 → 1), and the ADR 0012 simulation was run ([output-stage simulation](../../sim/output-stage/README.md)); the finding now applies to the boost inductor only, at the boost's limiting case (line 22), checked with the bench item of §7 item 29 |

| D33 | 106 | The XFL4015-471MEC saturation current (3.5 A at 10 % drop) is below the TPS62826 minimum switch limit (3.7 A) | Resolved: XFL4015 demoted; not an equivalent second source for the TPS62826 (BRD-049) |
| D34 | 106 | The 11.2 mΩ DCR quoted from TI's table is Würth's typical value; the datasheet maximum is 14 mΩ | Resolved: loss and thermal figures use 14 mΩ |
| D35 | 109 | LT3094 current-limit tolerance is −10 % / +12 %, not ±10 %: about 450 mA maximum at 9.31 kΩ (estimate) | Resolved: still inside the 500 mA rating and the downstream ratings; tolerance corrected in the margin table |
